《Governed Self-Evolution for Autonomous Investing: A Multi-Agent LLM System with an Invariant Core》归入 大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；实时应用对效率提出要求。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

LLM agents that act on live Web data must combine heterogeneous evidence, remember what they have done and improve from outcomes without weakening their own safeguards. We study this problem in autonomous investing, where every action is irreversible and based on untrusted Web content. We present Steward, a multi-agent LLM system that draws the boundary of self-evolution in its type system. First, nine role-specialised agents decide jointly: four analysts read news feeds, SEC filings, macroeconomic series and market data and emit typed reports, from which a Portfolio Manager sets target positions that a protected Risk Manager audits. Second, a seven-layer shared memory, assembled by code from the system's own record, gives each decision its position context, the market narrative, calibration and the agent's past decisions. Third, the system improves itself by review: code grades every trade and forecast nightly and writes the grades into memory, and each quarter a reflector proposes prompt learnings that a deterministic gate applies reversibly and replay of recorded inputs verifies. All three operate around an invariant core of order arithmetic, risk rules and discipline-agent prompts that no self-edit can reach. In a five-month live deployment (102 trading days, 1,632 LLM calls), the system contained a 24.6% sector drawdown to a 0.3% loss; in replay of its recorded inputs, a single model given the same raw data traded more and lost 1.35 points against the market over five days where the joint design showed no significant loss, and no injected instruction was followed; and a 2015-2026 backtest shows the core alone lowering the maximum drawdown under all 50 random entry policies. The reflector traced the dominant failure to its root cause, and the gate applied a learning that the agent adopted on trigger evenings and on no control evening. Code: https://github.com/yebof/quant-agent

</details>

#### 方法概述和架构

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Industry Foundation Classes (IFC), the standard for building information exchange in architecture, engineering, and construction (AEC), is increasingly queried through large language models (LLMs) using natural language. Evaluating these systems at scale is difficult because free-text answers require costly human or LLM-based review. We propose IFC-MCQ, a method that automatically generates multiple-choice benchmarks from IFC files for exact option-set grading. The taxonomy covers 15 categories and 36 subtypes across four groups, with answer keys derived from the file and distractors constructed from file-derived values or predefined rules. We applied IFC-MCQ to IFC models of varying sizes and modelling characteristics to assess generation coverage and evaluated two agentic configurations on selected models using tool- and code-based retrieval. The evaluation exposed category-specific failure modes and showed that performance can vary with the source IFC model and retrieval interface. IFC-MCQ enables scalable benchmarking of LLM-based IFC retrieval in AEC workflows.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/Why_Bother_Me_Again_Benchmarking_Personal_Agent_Memory_for_Interaction_Preferences|Why Bother Me Again? Benchmarking Personal Agent Memory for Interaction Preferences]]

![[assets/2610.30485_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2610.30485](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30485)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.30485
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/Why_Bother_Me_Again_Benchmarking_Personal_Agent_Memory_for_Interaction_Preferences|Why Bother Me Again? Benchmarking Personal Agent Memory for Interaction Preferences]]
- **作者**: Jialin Li, Zhenhao Chen, Hanjun Luo, Hanan Salam
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.CL
- **归属领域**: 大模型
- **相关领域**: 大模型
- **相关性评分**: 0.85（加权：大模型 0.85）
- **关联关键词**: LLM, Agent, RL

#### 研究背景与动机

《Why Bother Me Again? Benchmarking Personal Agent Memory for Interaction Preferences》归入 大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：AMemGym, MemPABench, PrefEval, VitaBench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Personal intelligence systems are taking on increasingly complex everyday tasks, yet repeated questions, unnecessary confirmations, and unwanted outreach can turn capable assistance into interaction burden. Reducing this burden requires agents to remember how users want to be assisted. However, existing memory benchmarks focus on factual recall and personalized answers and rarely test how agents learn and act on interaction preferences. We introduce MemPABench, an interactive environment for on-policy evaluation of memory in personal agents. MemPABench poses long-horizon tasks requiring agents to decide when to clarify, confirm, or act autonomously. Four grounded simulated users hold 14 preferences across work and personal contexts. Their feedback spans 99 sessions over four months, with five event-driven changes per user testing memory updates. We evaluate preference selection, delivered behavior, and sensitivity to context and preference change. Evaluating four memory systems across four LLM backbones, we find that current memory brings limited gains in preference selection. Retrieval-based systems (i.e. Mem0, MemOS, simple RAG) do not outperform No Memory, while File-based memory improves accuracy but applies preferences beyond relevant contexts. Our analysis shows that current memory struggles to retrieve interaction preferences and adapt to preference changes. Even with the correct preference available, overriding the agent's default interaction habits remains challenging. The environment and dataset are available at https://mempabench.com.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/Agent_Mosaic_Recognizing_Confidentiality_Leaks_in_Agentic_LLM_Systems|Agent Mosaic: Recognizing Confidentiality Leaks in Agentic LLM Systems]]

![[assets/2610.30418_first_page.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2610.30418](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30418)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.30418
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/Agent_Mosaic_Recognizing_Confidentiality_Leaks_in_Agentic_LLM_Systems|Agent Mosaic: Recognizing Confidentiality Leaks in Agentic LLM Systems]]
- **作者**: Daniel Ruiz, Luca Luceri, Emilio Ferrara
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.CL, cs.CR
- **归属领域**: 大模型
- **相关领域**: 大模型
- **相关性评分**: 0.95（加权：大模型 0.95）
- **关联关键词**: LLM, Agent, Systems

#### 研究背景与动机

《Agent Mosaic: Recognizing Confidentiality Leaks in Agentic LLM Systems》归入 大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入智能体式建模或搜索；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现；关注鲁棒性或泛化表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Pro-RL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Modern LLM agents can retrieve information from accessible documents, and then disclose a sensitive conclusion assembled from them. This mosaic effect creates a critical risk for agents operating over confidential data. We quantify its realization with 30 synthetic cybersecurity, medical, and military examples, yielding 10,800 final answers from three LLM agents in controlled simulation and 1,440 answers from live agents in two open-source harnesses. Even under benign queries designed to be answerable from one document, GPT-5.6-Sol, the best performing model tested, discloses forbidden composites in 7.08% of answers (95% CI: 3.75-10.67%) in simulation, with worse disclosure rates in live harnesses. Adding visible classification headers and explicit aggregation rules reduces pooled disclosure rates substantially, yet unsafe disclosures persist in 32.37% of simulated answers (95% CI: 28.33-36.19%) across all intents despite these safeguards. Furthermore, increased refusals do not guarantee confidentiality, since significant refusals reveal the prohibited conclusion regardless. These results point to a troubling conclusion: sensitive data leaks via mosaic-style events are an observed confidentiality failure mode in the evaluated settings at non-negligible rates. They motivate stronger controls on what agents may infer and disclose in sensitive data environments with mixed-clearance users.

</details>

- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/Edit,_Don't_Imitate_Minimal-Edit_On-Policy_Distillation_for_Multimodal_Reasoning|Edit, Don't Imitate: Minimal-Edit On-Policy Distillation for Multimodal Reasoning]]
- **作者**: Wen Ye, Ailin Deng, Zirui Wang, Shixing Yu, Han Zhang, Tian Yun, Nikhil Mehta, Tarun Kalluri, Xuefei Cao, Xuezhe Ma, Hengduo Li
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.LG
- **归属领域**: 大模型

- **关联关键词**: LLM, Multimodal, RL

#### 研究背景与动机

《Edit, Don't Imitate: Minimal-Edit On-Policy Distillation for Multimodal Reasoning》归入 大模型、强化学习、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 大模型、强化学习、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Visual mathematical reasoning can fail before reasoning begins: when a vision-language model (VLM) misreads a diagram, subsequent reasoning proceeds from false premises. This perceptual bottleneck is especially pronounced in smaller models, while accurate textual descriptions can substantially narrow the gap to larger models. Because obtaining such descriptions at inference requires additional annotation or model calls, we propose minimal-edit corrective on-policy distillation. Using reference figure descriptions and verified reference solutions as training-only information, a teacher scores incorrect student rollouts under a minimal-edit objective, and only teacher-disfavored tokens contribute to the corrective loss. Correct rollouts receive task-reward reinforcement learning. This preserves on-policy supervision while avoiding full-response imitation. Across model scales, the method requires no descriptions at test time and meets or exceeds the corresponding base model supplied with a reference figure description at inference. It also improves perception-oriented performance, particularly low-level geometric perception, and transfers to external mathematics and perception benchmarks. Because the minimal-edit teacher evaluates corrections rather than generating its own solution, the method remains effective with a frozen same-size teacher, although its gains are smaller than with a larger teacher on most metrics.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/Self-Evolving_Static_Analysis_via_LLM_Pseudo-Labeling|Self-Evolving Static Analysis via LLM Pseudo-Labeling]]

![[assets/2610.30184_figure.png|800]]

- **作者**: Rui Melo, Andre Duarte, Aditya Oke, Shubham Gandhi, Nachiket Kotalwar, Rui Abreu, Lei Li, Carolyn Rose
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.CR, cs.SE

#### 研究背景与动机

《Self-Evolving Static Analysis via LLM Pseudo-Labeling》归入 大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Security bugs often slip past static analysis not because they are impossible to detect, but because detection rules have not yet been written for the code in front of us. New frameworks, APIs, and coding idioms continually create such gaps, leaving developers to discover failures and manually encode new rules. We present SESAA, a framework in which an Large Language Model (LLM) agent starts from no rules and iteratively evolves a set of readable and auditable joern queries. The agent receives feedback only from a second LLM critic, which inspects flagged and unflagged code to identify likely false alarms and suspected misses, without access to ground-truth labels. The agent then uses this feedback to revise its queries. On the OWASP Benchmark for Python, SESAA reaches a mean F1 of 0.742 across 8 independent runs, well above the default configurations of five widely used tools and comparable to the strongest of them once it is tuned with access to ground-truth labels that SESAA never sees. In a pilot on real-world Python projects, queries induced from code available before a vulnerability was introduced detect 10 of 19 subsequent CVEs, including 9 detected by rules written for the same repository. The resulting detector is a set of executable, human-readable queries that engineers can inspect, modify, and run independently. These results suggest that LLM agents can turn static-analysis rule maintenance from a manually authored process into an iterative process of automated discovery and repair.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/Toward_Pre-Deployment_Assurance_for_Enterprise_AI_Agents_Ontology-Grounded_Simulation_and_Trust_Certification|Toward Pre-Deployment Assurance for Enterprise AI Agents: Ontology-Grounded Simulation and Trust Certification]]

![[assets/2606.04037_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2606.04037](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04037)

- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/Toward_Pre-Deployment_Assurance_for_Enterprise_AI_Agents_Ontology-Grounded_Simulation_and_Trust_Certification|Toward Pre-Deployment Assurance for Enterprise AI Agents: Ontology-Grounded Simulation and Trust Certification]]
- **作者**: Thanh Luong Tuan, Abhijit Sanyal
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.LG, cs.SE
- **归属领域**: 大模型
- **相关领域**: 大模型
- **相关性评分**: 1.0（加权：大模型 1）
- **关联关键词**: LLM, Agent, Security

#### 研究背景与动机

《Toward Pre-Deployment Assurance for Enterprise AI Agents: Ontology-Grounded Simulation and Trust Certification》归入 大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：A-Sim, ASSEBench, SafeAgentBench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Pre-deployment verification of enterprise artificial intelligence (AI) agents remains a critical gap between large language model (LLM) capability benchmarking and production deployment. Post-deployment monitoring, human-in-the-loop controls, and prompt-level guardrails offer limited assurance once an agent is operating in production. We present an ontology-grounded verification framework -- to our knowledge the first to combine three components: an Agent Operational Envelope formalizing the certification space across permissions, domain constraints, safety properties, governance rules, and autonomy levels; an ontology-to-scenario generation pipeline that derives regulatory, operational, and adversarial test scenarios automatically; and a machine-verifiable Trust Certificate with graduated deployment verdicts. A controlled pilot across four regulated industries (Fintech, Banking, Insurance, Healthcare), instantiated as five industry-by-regulatory-regime cells across the United States and Vietnam (where Vietnam's 2025 AI Law makes such verification legally mandated for financial services), generated 1,800 scenarios evaluated against 125 primary-source regulatory requirements and 25 injected faults. Ontology-grounded generation significantly outperformed the dominant persona-based baseline on regulatory coverage (48.3% versus 33.1%; corrected p_c = .0006) and attained the highest domain specificity (4.77/5.0; p = 2e-6); transparently, its advantage over plain and retrieval-augmented prompting did not survive Bonferroni correction. Cross-validation across three LLM families (Claude Sonnet 4, Qwen 2.5 72B, Gemma 4 26B; 5,400 total scenarios) replicated the persona-versus-ontology pattern. The framework offers a reproducible, regulation-grounded route to pre-deployment assurance for enterprise AI agents, complementing runtime governance with an auditable deployment gate.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/ChemBART_Pre-training_a_Reaction_Language_Model_for_Comprehensive_Synthesis_Planning|ChemBART: Pre-training a Reaction Language Model for Comprehensive Synthesis Planning]]

![[assets/2601.02915_first_page.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2601.02915](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.02915)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2601.02915
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/大模型/ChemBART_Pre-training_a_Reaction_Language_Model_for_Comprehensive_Synthesis_Planning|ChemBART: Pre-training a Reaction Language Model for Comprehensive Synthesis Planning]]
- **作者**: Kenan Li, Yijian Zhang, Jin Wang, Haipeng Gan, Zeying Sun, Xiaoguang Lei, Hao Dong
- **cs 子类**: cs.CL, cs.LG
- **归属领域**: 大模型
- **相关领域**: 大模型, 强化学习, 世界模型
- **相关性评分**: 1.17（加权：大模型 0.65，强化学习 0.36，世界模型 0.16）
- **关联关键词**: LLM, Agent, RL

#### 研究背景与动机

《ChemBART: Pre-training a Reaction Language Model for Comprehensive Synthesis Planning》归入 大模型、强化学习、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Computation and Language 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：该工作聚焦 Computation and Language 方向中的具体问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 大模型、强化学习、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated transformative potential across diverse fields. However, in computer-aided synthesis planning (CASP), current LLM methodologies are often restricted to single-molecule representations, limiting their application to isolated, single-task scenarios such as precursor prediction. Addressing this gap, we present ChemBART, a BART-based language model that adopts a reaction-centric pre-training strategy. By leveraging a mask-filling pre-training task on holistic reaction expressions (Reactant &gt; Reagent &gt; Product), ChemBART effectively solves a variety of chemical problems, including precursor/reagent generation, temperature-yield regression, and molecular property classification. Building on these capabilities, we demonstrate the integration of ChemBART with Monte Carlo tree search (MCTS) for multi-step synthesis route design, where the model serves to optimize the policy and value functions within a reinforcement learning framework. Beyond single-molecule pre-trained LLMs that are constrained to specific applications, ChemBART unifies diverse chemical tasks to facilitate synthesis planning with comprehensive implementation information. Most significantly, the proposed multi-step synthesis routes and reaction conditions were validated in the wet lab, confirming the discovery of shorter synthetic pathways with a &gt;20% yield improvement over established literature benchmarks. Our work highlights the potential of reaction-focused pre-training and showcases the broad utility of ChemBART in closing the loop between in silico prediction and wet-lab synthesis.

</details>

《METTA: Memory-Enhanced Test-Time Adaptation for Meta-Reinforcement Learning》归入 强化学习、世界模型、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、世界模型、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Meta-RL, Meta-World, TrMRL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Meta-reinforcement learning aims to train agents that adapt quickly to unseen tasks from limited interaction, but this requires representing task evidence over long interaction histories. Classical recurrent agents compress experience into hidden states, while transformer-based agents retrieve from past trajectories at quadratic memory and computation cost. In this work, we propose Memory-Enhanced Test-Time Adaptation (METTA) for meta-reinforcement learning. It decomposes the fast adaptation phase into two temporal scales: a causal neural memory performs fine-grained within-episode adaptation with linear sequence complexity, while compact episode-level summaries support long-range cross-episode task inference without attending over the full interaction history. Moreover, equipping agents with neural memory provides sufficient expressive capacity and test-time training capability, enabling effective adaptation to unseen held-out tasks and task families at meta-test time. On unseen held-out Meta-World ML10/45 test environments, METTA achieves a success rate over $50\%$ while maintaining a low training budget and short wall-clock time. On long-context sparse-reward PointMaze tasks, it outperforms transformer-based baselines by exhibiting more structured exploration behavior. These results show that METTA provides an efficient path toward more adaptive and scalable long-context meta-RL.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/Same_Luck,_Different_Choice_Twin-World_Credit_Assignment_for_Agentic_Reinforcement_Learning|Same Luck, Different Choice: Twin-World Credit Assignment for Agentic Reinforcement Learning]]

![[assets/2610.31544_figure.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2610.31544](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31544)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31544
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/Same_Luck,_Different_Choice_Twin-World_Credit_Assignment_for_Agentic_Reinforcement_Learning|Same Luck, Different Choice: Twin-World Credit Assignment for Agentic Reinforcement Learning]]
- **作者**: Zeropy Surio, Tobias Engel, Mei-Lin Chou, Anika Raman, Samuel Okafor
- **cs 子类**: cs.LG
- **归属领域**: 强化学习
- **相关领域**: 强化学习, 世界模型
- **相关性评分**: 1.12（加权：强化学习 0.96，世界模型 0.16）
- **关联关键词**: RL

#### 研究背景与动机

《Same Luck, Different Choice: Twin-World Credit Assignment for Agentic Reinforcement Learning》归入 强化学习、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入智能体式建模或搜索；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Twin-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Agentic reinforcement learning rewards a complete episode, yet the policy needs credit for every decision inside it. Counterfactual branching estimates this credit by re-running the episode after an alternative decision, but the branch draws its sampling noise independently of the factual episode, so the outcome difference mixes the effect of the decision with the luck of everything that follows. We observe that in agentic search the retriever, the corpus, and the grader are deterministic, so an episode is a deterministic function of the policy's decisions and its sampling noise. Twin worlds are therefore realizable: the same noise, a different decision. We introduce TWICE (TWIn-world Credit Estimation), which keys the Gumbel noise of every token by turn and line, rolls out counterfactual twins that inherit the factual episode's downstream noise, and turns their paired outcome differences into turn-level advantages for policy-gradient updates on actor-sampled trajectories; only the actor is deployed. Twin credit is unbiased for any coupling quality, its variance is that of an independent counterfactual scaled by $1-ρ$, where $ρ$ is the twins' outcome correlation, and under coupling the number of rollouts needed to resolve an advantage of size $Δ$ falls from $Θ(Δ^{-2})$ to $Θ(Δ^{-1})$. A square-root rule spends the twin budget where outcomes are predicted to disagree per unit cost. On seven question-answering benchmarks, TWICE improves the average exact match of GRPO by 5.3 and 4.5 points on Qwen2.5-3B and 7B, outperforms tree-search and counterfactual-branching baselines, and uses 68% fewer auxiliary tokens than VinePPO.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Cross-Region Cooperation of UAV Swarms Towards Air-Ground ISAC via Hierarchical Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning》归入 强化学习、机器人、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 cs.OH 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、机器人、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：DRL, H-MARL, MARL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

The limited communication resources and sensing perspectives of a single uncrewed aerial vehicle (UAV) motivate UAV swarms for integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) in air-ground networks. However, in multi-region scenarios, regional UAV allocations may not match vehicle demand, while local observations may lack sufficient diversity for accurate sensing. To address these issues, this paper proposes a cross-region cooperation framework for UAV swarms. For communication, cross-region UAV reallocation reduces service disparities by matching resources to vehicle demand. For sensing, combining UAV observations from different regions increases observation diversity, while adaptive handshaking guided by marginal Cramér--Rao bound (CRB) utilities balances phase synchronization gains against handshaking energy consumption. Joint optimization of UAV positions and transmit power within each region then further improves communication service and sensing performance. These coupled decisions are coordinated by a hierarchical multi-agent reinforcement learning (H-MARL) approach through slow-timescale inter-region UAV reallocation and fast-timescale intra-region UAV control. Under the considered simulation settings, reallocation can improve quality of service (QoS) by over 20 percentage points compared with retaining the initial allocation under severe resource-demand mismatches. Cooperative sensing with adaptive handshaking incurs only about a 5.0% increase in mean CRB relative to perfect synchronization.

</details>

![[assets/2610.31037_figure.jpg|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2610.31037](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.31037)

- **作者**: Jichen Zhu, Chaopeng Tan
- **cs 子类**: 
- **归属领域**: 强化学习

#### 研究背景与动机

《Stability-Preserving Reinforcement Learning for Heterogeneity-Aware Composite Max-Pressure Traffic Signal Control》归入 强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 cs.OH 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；实时应用对效率提出要求；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：设计端到端框架；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开；使用优化建模或搜索过程。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Reinforcement learning (RL) provides a flexible mechanism for adapting traffic signal control to heterogeneous traffic and sensing conditions, but directly learned signal actions do not automatically inherit the analytical properties of structured traffic-control methods. This paper proposes Com-MP, a theory-guided RL framework that learns a context-dependent composition of multiple max-pressure (MP) control preferences while retaining decentralized MP phase selection. The learned policy adapts the relative influence of constituent MP formulations according to local traffic and sensing conditions, allowing data-driven adaptation to operate within a structured traffic-control framework. We show that such intersection-specific and time-varying composition preserves strong queue stability for every demand in the strictly stabilizable region under any admissible non-anticipative composition policy, thereby separating the performance-adaptation role of learning from the stability property of the underlying controller. In a representative implementation combining detector-supported queue-based MP and connected-vehicle-supported travel-time MP, experiments on a real-world 45-intersection network show that Com-MP reduces average delay by 15.6\% and 8.6\% relative to the two constituent controllers, respectively, and by about 7.0\% relative to the best fixed composition. The learned composition responds systematically to local traffic and sensing conditions, remains effective under heterogeneous and degraded sensing, and can be transferred without retraining to an unseen network with an average delay only 2.4\% higher than that of a policy trained directly on the target network. These results show how learning-based adaptation can be introduced within a stability-preserving traffic-control structure, providing a theory-guided pathway toward trustworthy learning-enabled traffic signal control.

</details>

- **作者**: Sanjiv Das, Harshad Khadilkar, Sukrit Mittal, Daniel Ostrov, Jacob Sisk

#### 研究背景与动机

《CARL: Chat Assisted Reinforcement Learning》归入 强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Human-Computer Interaction 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入智能体式建模或搜索；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开；使用优化建模或搜索过程。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：CARL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Failure of applied Reinforcement Learning (RL) to solve a given problem predominantly stems from unaddressed specification gaps, where the intent of human experts in the problem's domain is inaccurately or incompletely translated by AI into mathematical objectives, constraints, and reward functions. Failure can also stem from unaddressed performance gaps, where human domain experts are unable to determine the validity of the RL based solution or where parameters (i.e., hyperparameters or weights on the reward terms comprising the reward) are poorly chosen. Chat Assisted Reinforcement Learning (CARL) bridges these gaps through a human-AI co-design interface architecture that enforces interactive specification prior to code generation and then enables subsequent verification and parameter alterations via iteration. More specifically, CARL leads domain experts through a structured ten-step workflow for each iteration using step-specific skill files, domain-aware suggestions via an assistant persona (called Trillian), and compact decision summaries that maintain context across iterations without context overload. Once the domain expert confirms the Markov decision process (MDP) specified by this procedure, CARL's autonomous tool-use loop generates, tests, executes, and debugs an end-to-end RL training pipeline, followed by an interactive post-training diagnosis and revisit step that allows for verification and parameter alteration before running a new iteration if desired.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Ground-Truth-First Benchmarking of Reinforcement Learning via Converse Optimality: From Smooth, Fully Observed Systems to Non-Smooth or Partially Observed Tasks》归入 强化学习、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；达到作者报告的目标性能；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：D4RL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Benchmarking in reinforcement learning is usually relative: algorithms are ranked by returns on environments with unknown optima. Distance to optimality remains unobservable, while conclusions may depend on reward design, stochasticity, hyperparameters, environment selection, and simulator details. We develop a ground-truth-first framework based on converse optimality, constructing each task with a certified optimal value function and policy withheld from learners and used only for evaluation. The framework unifies two modes: sensitivity benchmarking samples certified environments from continuous-time generators to assess robustness to task variation, while oracle-referenced comparison uses native discrete-time generators to evaluate policies via optimality gap, regret, and policy error. Continuous-time models support physically interpretable sensitivity studies but introduce dependence on the simulator step ($Δt$) and numerical scheme. Native discrete-time models align the environment, oracle, and learner on the same transition law, eliminating external integration choices as a confounder. We reproduce quantitative results covering optimizer sensitivity and oracle-referenced comparisons on serial planar arms, dynamically extended nonholonomic vehicles, and quadrotor-inspired tasks. The methodology extends beyond smooth affine Gaussian systems: virtual-input pullback handles non-affine raw-action interfaces and finite-covariance non-Gaussian disturbances. A non-smooth converse optimality formulation enables certified benchmarks with kinked value functions and switching surfaces. For partially observed evaluation, an information-state formulation reduces benchmarking to converse optimality on a sufficient statistic and provides an information-penalty reference. Code, fixtures, and diagnostics are available on the public project page.

</details>

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：ReflecTool-Bench, TRAJECT-Bench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

With external tools, large language models (LLMs) can tackle complex tasks through sustained environmental interaction. LLM-based agents are therefore increasingly used to tackle complex long-horizon tasks in real-world environments. However, these tasks involve sequential, interdependent decisions and changes in environmental states. Early execution errors affect subsequent interaction histories and environmental states, and their effects accumulate along the decision chain. To address this challenge, we propose Reflection-Guided Policy Optimization (ReflectPO), a reinforcement learning framework for autonomous error recovery in long-horizon tasks. ReflectPO comprises two core components. The first is Reflection-Guided Sampling. We use branch differences and repeated visits in interaction trajectories to construct hints for error recovery. The second is Tree-Structured Credit Assignment. We organize sampled trajectories into trees and assign action-level credit using parent--child reward differences and recovery gains. Experiments over two long-horizon tool-use benchmarks show that ReflectPO outperforms multiple existing baselines in most domains. It also achieves the best overall performance on average. Further analyses show that ReflectPO effectively improves agents' ability to recognize their own deviations, recover from erroneous states, and continue making progress toward task completion.

</details>

![[assets/2610.30882_first_page.png|800]]

- **作者**: Zhizhuo Kou, Sizai Hou, Long Chen, Jian Yang, Junyu Luo, Jingshu Peng, Wenkai Zhang, Sirui Han, Yike Guo
- **cs 子类**: cs.CL, cs.LG

#### 研究背景与动机

《Inherited, Not Invented: Tracing the Provenance of Wait in Reasoning Models》归入 强化学习、大模型、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Computation and Language 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束；鲁棒性和泛化能力是核心问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、大模型、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Pre-RL, Zero-RL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Reinforcement learning (RL) is credited with teaching reasoning models to write {wait} before reconsidering a step, yet large RL accuracy gains do not require the word. This view can confuse what RL discovers with what a model inherits from its training data. We trace the word through open models' training histories, reading it with label-free indices of frequency, position, form and usage and an injected-error test, all computed from sampled outputs. We find that zero-RL almost never coins the marker: on bases that lack the word, RL raises MATH-500 accuracy substantially while {wait} stays near absent. It arrives with training data: mid-training in OLMo-3, and fine-tuning on reasoning traces installs it in most models whose base lacks it. Where the base already uses it, both released RL-Zero recipes (OLMo-3, MiMo) multiply it, whereas KL-free GRPO from the same base does not; OLMo-3's recipe wears down its form and makes it a more sensitive but more liberal alarm; RL after supervised fine-tuning barely changes it, and our GRPO recipe erases a marker seeded by fine-tuning. Finally, we show that marker frequency alone does not indicate RL-induced reasoning and caution against LLM-as-a-judge revision metrics, as automated judges can reverse human-labelled trends.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/MOER_Mix-Policy_Optimization_under_Expert_Prefix_Guidance_with_Selective_Rollback|MOER: Mix-Policy Optimization under Expert Prefix Guidance with Selective Rollback]]

![[assets/2610.30873_first_page.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2610.30873](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30873)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.30873
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/MOER_Mix-Policy_Optimization_under_Expert_Prefix_Guidance_with_Selective_Rollback|MOER: Mix-Policy Optimization under Expert Prefix Guidance with Selective Rollback]]
- **作者**: Xuyang Zhong, Jiuding Yang, Yiwen Guo, Chen Liu
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI, cs.LG
- **归属领域**: 强化学习
- **相关领域**: 强化学习, 世界模型
- **相关性评分**: 1.32（加权：强化学习 1.16，世界模型 0.16）
- **关联关键词**: RL

#### 研究背景与动机

《MOER: Mix-Policy Optimization under Expert Prefix Guidance with Selective Rollback》归入 强化学习、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：SFT-RL, SFT-then-RL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Mix-policy optimization incorporates off-policy expert data into the reinforcement learning (RL) loop to transcend the capability boundary of on-policy RL. However, existing methods either provide static full-trace guidance that suppresses self-exploration, or adopt expert prefix guidance without correctness guarantees, leading to suboptimal performance or even training collapse. In this paper, we propose MOER, a mix-policy optimization framework that harmonizes expert guidance with self-exploration. MOER injects a decaying expert chain-of-thought prefix into the prompt and lets the model complete the remaining reasoning, with the prefix length progressively annealed to encourage self-exploration. A selective rollback mechanism replaces failed completions with the original full expert trace, guaranteeing the correctness of every off-policy trajectory. Furthermore, a harmonized mix-policy objective optimizes on-policy tokens with a GRPO loss while supervising both expert prefix and completion tokens with a trust-region SFT loss, substantially stabilizing training. Extensive experiments show that MOER achieves the highest average scores in mathematical and general domain reasoning tasks, outperforming all basic and mix-policy baselines, and generalizes consistently across diverse tasks, model scales, and base model categories. Codes are available at https://github.com/CityU-MLO/MOER.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Trace2World: Reconstructing App Worlds from Real Interaction Traces for Scalable GUI Agent Training》归入 强化学习、大模型、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入智能体式建模或搜索；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、大模型、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；关注鲁棒性或泛化表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：Agent-World, AndroidWorld, AppWorld, CUA-Gym, MobileGym, PhoneWorld。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Scaling GUI reinforcement learning requires environments that preserve real-app behavior. Yet coupling behavior inference with coding can turn partial traces into runnable mocks with incorrect state transitions. We introduce \textbf{Trace2World}, which separates global behavior reconstruction from implementation. It reconciles traces into a shared, source-linked app model, then compiles an executable environment. Independent replay of source workflows localizes mismatches for bounded implementation repair. Validated worlds then support learner-adaptive task and environment augmentation within source-supported behavior. From 1,570 traces, Trace2World constructs 50 environments. On 200 workflows across ten apps, 91.1% are reproduced end to end, versus 59.8% for the strongest matched task-local baseline. Source-grounded validation retains 94.0% of simulated training gains on real devices, versus 54.7% under self-validation. Learner guidance raises benchmark success from 56.9% to 61.1%. Training in Trace2World further improves the model by 20.2 points on our benchmark, 5.4--16.2 points across four external GUI benchmarks, and 16.3 points on 100 real-device tasks. Reliable reconstruction separates what behavior source evidence supports from whether the implementation preserves it.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/Fast_Iterative_Combinatorial_Auctions_via_Deep_Reinforcement_Learning|Fast Iterative Combinatorial Auctions via Deep Reinforcement Learning]]

#### 研究背景与动机

《Fast Iterative Combinatorial Auctions via Deep Reinforcement Learning》归入 强化学习、世界模型 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开；使用优化建模或搜索过程。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、世界模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Iterative combinatorial auctions are used in spectrum allocation, transportation, and industrial procurement, but rounds are costly. We introduce FastCombo, a deep reinforcement learning approach to sequential price discovery. Bayesian pricing has sharply reduced auction rounds even against subgradient benchmarks tuned with hindsight for each instance, but relies on a one-step objective and repeated online inference and optimization. We prove that greedily maximizing immediate clearing probability can require arbitrarily more rounds in expectation than an informative policy, even with the true valuation distribution known. FastCombo learns price policies offline, assigning credit to queries for the future decisions they enable and producing each online price update with a single neural-network forward pass. We formulate the objective of minimizing expected rounds to clearing in single-minded auctions as a partially observable Markov decision process and train a recurrent bidder-item graph policy over complete auction trajectories. In a one-item experiment, all five trained policies learn an informative opening, reducing expected rounds from 1.85 to 1.65 relative to the tested Bayesian one-step rule. Across four domains from the Combinatorial Auction Test Suite, FastCombo achieves competitive clearing while reducing measured online price-selection time per auction by a factor of roughly 300-900 relative to the standard Bayesian baseline. These results demonstrate the value of learning sequential price policies for both information acquisition and online computational efficiency.

</details>

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Financial news can influence investment decisions long after publication. Timely news may reach an investor before prices fully adjust, whereas older news may already be reflected in prices. We examine two ways to handle aging signals: retain them and give a portfolio policy timing information, or filter them out before portfolio selection. A fixed large language model (LLM) extracts signals from public SEC disclosures. We compare these treatments in reinforcement learning (RL) policies and LLMs that choose portfolios directly. We evaluate returns after trading costs in historical tests over 2023-2025. RL choices respond to timing information in a controlled test, but historical returns show no consistent benefit from adding it. For the LLM, filtering old news produced the highest average net return, reaching 18.43% per year when news delivery was delayed by four weeks. This exceeded the returns of all RL strategies that used news.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Learning to Incentivise: Using Reinforcement Learning for Sustainable Urban Mobility》归入 强化学习、世界模型、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、世界模型、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：MARL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Traffic management has traditionally focused on toll-based and road pricing solutions. However, road pricing often raises concerns about accessibility and public dissatisfaction, leading to its prohibition in some places, such as Finland. This study optimises the dynamic allocation of incentives to drivers, encouraging them to reroute onto alternative (potentially longer) paths to achieve greater societal benefit, reduced total travel time (TTT) and total emissions in the transportation network, contributing to sustainable urban mobility. We employ a multi-agent reinforcement learning approach to dynamically assign incentives to drivers to reduce both TTT and emissions, with travel times estimated using traffic simulation software. We demonstrate that, with an unlimited budget and an objective of minimising travel time, the incentive scheme reduces TTT by 16%, compared to the dynamic User-Equilibrium (UE) with a budget equivalent to about 11% of the UE total time. When the goal is to minimise emissions, a 9% reduction in CO2 emissions is observed under an unlimited budget. We demonstrate a critical trade-off: minimising TTT leads to an increase in emissions, while prioritising emission reductions raises TTT. However, with the right combination of weights in the multi-objective function, both TTT and total emissions are improved beyond the baseline.

</details>

《Strategic Arbitrage in Electricity Markets: A Multi-Agent Policy Optimization Framework》归入 强化学习、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 cs.OH 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Grid-scale storage and load aggregators are growing quickly in wholesale electricity markets. If highly consolidated, these new participants may distort the competitive market equilibrium through strategic withholding behavior. Traditional market power mitigation techniques are ill-suited to arbitrage-based participants. We provide a multi-agent policy optimization model to study game-theoretic equilibria for real-time markets with consolidated storage and/or aggregators. The model is designed to facilitate natural first-order equilibrium-seeking update rules. We analyze the theoretical properties of different supply functions and their implications on the convergence of the gradient dynamics. Finally, we train equilibrium policies for a modified version of the IEEE RTS-GMLC benchmark across different levels of storage fleet consolidation, to demonstrate the potential of the framework as a tool to characterize and quantify strategic arbitrage.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《SpecMAGIC: Specification-Guided Multi-Task Generalization in Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning》归入 强化学习、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：相对已有方法取得更好表现；在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：ACC-MARL, MARL, MTMARL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Multi-task generalization is an important yet underexplored problem in multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL). Many real-world applications require multi-agent systems to generalize across complex, temporally extended tasks with diverse inter-agent coordination requirements. We introduce a specification language that combines temporal-logic task specifications with operators for expressing such requirements. We propose SpecMAGIC, a hierarchical framework that leverages the automaton structure of task specifications for subtask decomposition. A centralized high-level planner identifies and allocates compatible subtask sequences across agents, while decentralized low-level policies execute individual subtasks. This enables zero-shot generalization to new tasks by composing reusable subtask policies according to new specifications, without retraining. We establish soundness and probabilistic completeness of the high-level planner. Experiments on multi-agent navigation tasks show that SpecMAGIC substantially outperforms existing approaches on unseen task specifications and maintains strong performance as the number of agents increases.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Self-Assessment Reinforcement Learning for Search-State Awareness in Retrieval-Augmented Large Language Models》归入 强化学习、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：任务本身具有较高难度；相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 强化学习、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：HotpotQA, SARL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Recent advances in search agents have enabled large language models to tackle complex tasks by retrieving and synthesizing external information. However, existing search agents often lack search-state awareness, i.e., the ability to recognize what information has been obtained and what remains missing, leading to hallucinated reasoning and prematurely terminated search. To address this limitation, we propose Self-Assessment Reinforcement Learning (SARL), a reinforcement learning framework in which models learn to recognize their own search state during training and improve their search behavior accordingly, without requiring additional assessment at test time. SARL achieves this through a dual reward design that captures the corrective effect of each assessment while penalizing excessive reliance on it, jointly encouraging effective and efficient search trajectories. Under this setup, SARL achieves a 24.3% relative improvement over the strongest baseline, with average gains of 35.9% on multi-hop reasoning and 27.9% on deep research benchmarks against the strongest per-benchmark baselines. SARL further surpasses methods built on auxiliary supervision or multi-agent coordination, despite operating as a standalone RL framework with no inference-time scaffolding. Beyond final performance, SARL maintains training stability under extended search horizons, a regime where existing methods consistently degrade, enabling sustained gains as task complexity grows.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《DiffDreamer: Coupling World Models with Differentiable Simulation for Agile Quadrotor Control》归入 世界模型、机器人、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；实时应用对效率提出要求。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、机器人、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：DiffRL, Real-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Tasks such as quadrotor dynamic-gate racing require agents to anticipate external motion while maintaining precise high-frequency control. World-model-based reinforcement learning provides a natural solution by learning latent dynamics for future imagination, yet it often entangles environmental evolution and the robot's own dynamics in a single learned rollout, where model bias can affect policy optimization efficiency. We propose DiffDreamer, a differentiable reinforcement learning framework that tightly couples a learned world model with differentiable simulation for actor optimization. In one optimization step, the learned world model propagates latent states that capture external dynamics and history-dependent features, while the differentiable simulator propagates the robot's explicit physical state. We further decompose the actor objective into a critic-driven pathway for non-differentiable task rewards and a simulator-driven pathway for differentiable motion rewards. Experiments on aggressive tracking and dynamic-gate racing show that DiffDreamer improves training convergence over the model-free, model-based, and differentiable-RL baselines, and final performance over these baselines and classical planning and control methods. Additional experiments assess reward decomposition and the odometry-conditioned relative-state representation through ablations. We also provide proof-of-concept real-world flight experiments for validation.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Inside-JEPA: From Prediction to Relational Grounding in Latent World Models》归入 世界模型、机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：该工作聚焦 Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition 方向中的具体问题。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：ImageNet, ResNet。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) learn world models in latent space, enabling efficient planning but leaving their states opaque. We introduce Inside-JEPA, which places relational channels for ordered entity pairs inside the JEPA latent state and shows that an inverse-dynamics loss can ground these channels without relation labels when its head reads them, either alone or as part of the full state. On Something-Something V2, latent prediction alone performs no better than a random relation head, while inverse-dynamics supervision that recovers actions from channel changes improves relational AUROC by .065-.103 across ten settings with annotated boxes, by .081 on RH20T robot episodes with held-out tasks, and by .050-.061 across five relations using off-the-shelf detection and tracking. An inverse head on entity latents decodes actions more accurately but barely improves relational grounding; shuffled labels, two label-free objectives, and action labels on only 5% of the pairs also fail to ground the channels. Given an action, predicted relational changes approach the quality of observed future relations (AUROC .599 vs .621). For one-step relational goals, a supervised readout of the 16 channels ranks the correct action among the top five of 65 in 43% of cases, compared with 24% for prediction-only latents and 17% for a frequency prior.

</details>

《Dual Exam: Controlled Evaluation of Multimodal Agents and World Models》归入 世界模型、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

World Models (WMs) can support Multimodal Agents (MAs) through simulated experience, while Agent trajectories can inform World Model refinement. Evaluating this interaction requires separate evidence about action selection and state evolution. We introduce Dual Exam, which tests Agents under fixed environment response mechanisms and World Models under fixed action interventions across physical, digital, social, and scientific domains. The Agent track compares 10 configurations on 1,088 items in 15 reporting categories. The World Model track contains 400 cases across eight scenarios; 15 configurations have full coverage and one covers six scenarios. GPT 6 Astra leads the case-weighted Agent Index at 31.3, and GPT-Image-2 leads the full-coverage WM Overall at 21.7. These scores use different tasks and metrics and are not directly comparable. A 150-case pilot of one coupled pairing records zero task successes and motivates component-level diagnosis. Results reveal uneven task performance and failures to preserve task-relevant state. Their value for predicting coupled success or sustained reciprocal learning remains to be established.

</details>

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

World models are increasingly used to reduce environment interaction when post-training vision-language-action (VLA) policies with reinforcement learning. Yet they are typically trained to predict pixels one step ahead, even though their role here is not merely to render visually faithful videos but to produce learning signals from imagined rollouts that improve the policy. We show that this mismatch is substantial: lower pixel MSE can produce worse policy gradients, and a world model trained for policy utility can yield better downstream policies despite 17% higher pixel MSE on the next frame. We propose the Downstream-Return Imagination Learning Loop (DRILL), a bilevel framework that updates the world model to maximize the policy's return after an inner GRPO update on imagined rollouts. DRILL has two instantiations. DRILL-VWI is a closed-form surrogate, requiring no additional simulator rollouts, that reweights prediction errors by policy visitation, the group-relative GRPO advantage, and model confidence. DRILL-IMG is a full meta-gradient method that differentiates through the inner update using truncated Hessian-vector products. We show that DRILL-VWI recovers the local one-step component of the DRILL-IMG meta-gradient under score-compatibility and small-residual assumptions. Across five manipulation simulators and two VLA backbones, DRILL-IMG raises mean success rate by 13.1 points over WMPO and 7.9 points over RLVR-World, and complements RLVR pretraining rather than competing with it. More broadly, we believe video world models for VLA reinforcement learning can be productively trained by the policy gradients they produce, not only by pixel-level prediction.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/An_Introduction_to_World_Models_for_Generalist_Agents|An Introduction to World Models for Generalist Agents]]

![[assets/2610.30789_first_page.png|800]]

#### 研究背景与动机

《An Introduction to World Models for Generalist Agents》归入 世界模型、大模型、强化学习 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索；围绕策略学习或控制策略展开。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、大模型、强化学习 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；结果验证了方案可行性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

World models have recently emerged as a promising direction toward developing generalist agents capable of understanding, predicting, and interacting with diverse environments. However, despite growing interest, the concept of a world model remains loosely defined, encompassing approaches ranging from predictive dynamics models and learned simulators to models that jointly generate future observations and actions. This introductory paper aims to clarify the concept of world models and provide a unified perspective on their role in developing generalist agents. Starting from the partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP) framework, we connect world modeling to classical control, dynamical systems, signal processing, and reinforcement learning. We introduce different approaches to world modeling, emphasizing the knowledge they capture, their learning objectives, and how they support decision-making. We further explore the relationship between world modeling and policy learning, highlighting how these capabilities can be unified through conditional prediction without requiring separate models. Rather than offering an exhaustive survey of existing methods, this paper focuses on fundamental concepts, modeling principles, and connections, with the goal of providing an accessible and unified introduction to world models for generalist agents.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《Anticipating Social Consequences with Distributional World Models》归入 世界模型、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Social world models predict how interactions develop after an action, helping agents negotiate, cooperate and mediate. In these situations, one action can lead to several plausible futures, hence predicting only one future can overlook consequential alternatives, while exploring them through repeated simulations increases inference cost. We propose DSWM, a Distributional Social World Model that predicts alternative responses, their probabilities and immediate and final outcome values in one output per candidate action. We train a 4B model on repeated simulations using supervised fine-tuning and GRPO. At inference time, the actor uses these possible consequences to choose an action. Across three seen and two unseen environments, with interaction backbones absent from training collection, DSWM achieves the best average performance on 13 of 15 metrics. Ablations show that retaining multiple futures and their predicted probabilities improves outcomes in all five environments.

</details>

![[assets/2610.30488_first_page.png|800]]

- **arXiv**: [2610.30488](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30488)
- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.30488
- **详细分析**: [[20_Research/Papers/强化学习/From_Single-Actor_Environments_to_Multi-Actor_Event_Worlds_Can_LLMs_Be_Event_World_Models|From Single-Actor Environments to Multi-Actor Event Worlds: Can LLMs Be Event World Models?]]
- **作者**: Juntong Ni, Zewen Liu, Bohan Wang, Lige Zhang, Wei Jin
- **cs 子类**: cs.AI
- **归属领域**: 世界模型
- **相关领域**: 世界模型, 大模型

#### 研究背景与动机

《From Single-Actor Environments to Multi-Actor Event Worlds: Can LLMs Be Event World Models?》归入 世界模型、大模型 方向。该论文围绕 Artificial Intelligence 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；任务本身具有较高难度；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 世界模型、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：ALFWorld, EventWorld, StableToolBench。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

World models help agents assess the consequences of their actions before acting. Many real systems, however, involve multiple actors who make their own decisions, can evolve even when the agent takes no action, and are only partially observed through event logs. We formalize these systems as event worlds and ask whether large language models can predict future observations from the log and the agent's proposed actions. We build complementary benchmarks from human Diplomacy games and real Polymarket trades. Our hierarchical framework compares prediction with and without other actors' actions, then tests multi-step rollouts, predictions for actions never taken, and usefulness to agents. Fine-tuning and more data improve prediction when all actors' recorded actions are given. With only the agent's action, models struggle when other actors strongly affect the outcome, and larger models and longer histories help little. This gap also limits multi-step prediction and agent decisions, with models overestimating plan success. Yet imagined experience and initialization from world models can still improve policy learning. Anticipating other actors' actions remains a central challenge for event world modeling.

</details>

#### 研究背景与动机

《OpenTac: An Open Ecosystem for Customizable, Scalable, and Transferable 3D Tactile Sensing》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：给出系统化方法或工具；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：OpenTac-Sim。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

We present OpenTac, a novel sensor and a full-stack ecosystem for tactile skins that are highly customizable, scalable, and readily deployed in downstream robot control and learning applications. Our core contribution is twofold. First, we develop a scalable resistive tactile skin with a structured deformable overlay that converts tangential loading into spatially varying pressure. Differential responses across neighboring "taxels" support decoding of local shear vectors, and the common-mode response encodes normal force. Second, we provide a highly customizable sensor design tool that is able to automatically generate fabrication files for these tactile skins from 2D outlines and developable 3D surfaces, including from abstract descriptions such as text (e.g., "design a sensor for a size 9 shoe insole"). We develop an ecosystem around the OpenTac sensor, including the CAD design tool, a sensor simulator, cross-sensor transfer, and fast/adaptive data acquisition. We demonstrate the broad applicability of the OpenTac-CAD sensor design tool by designing and fabricating more than 30 sensors across a variety of robot embodiments. We characterize the three-dimensional tactile fields produced by OpenTac with a commercial, high-precision force/torque sensor. We demonstrate the broad applicability of 3D tactile sensing across contact-rich feedback control and manipulation policy learning on four real-world robot embodiments. Additionally, adaptive acquisition enables kilohertz-rate partial readouts, exceeding full-array raster scanning on the same sensor by 19.2X at 16 sampled taxels per update for a 1024-taxel skin. We provide all hardware, software, and result videos are at opentac.github.io

</details>

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：GRL, RRL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

We consider the offline generation of coordinated paths for N interchangeable robots and N goals in planar environments with polygonal obstacles. After a conformal map to a circular domain, starts and goals become the poles and zeros of a rational function R, and the paths are the branches of its Generalized Root Locus (GRL), i.e. the streamlines of the logarithmic potential -log R. We show that, for every phase outside a finite set of critical phases and a set of boundary phases, the induced gradient motion drives each robot to a distinct goal along pairwise disjoint paths that never reach the obstacles; the robot-goal assignment is produced by the construction, and with the gain used as a clock all robots move as the closed-loop roots of the GRL and arrive together at a prescribed time. Obstacles become streamlines through successive Milne-Thomson reflections, and for a finite number of reflections a finite test certifies safety for the potential that is actually computed. Reciprocal root-locus planning is recovered as a special case. The method is illustrated on two polygonal workspaces, 100 random instances compared with independent harmonic planning and artificial potentials, and a scene with ten robots and ten obstacles.

</details>

### [[20_Research/Papers/具身智能/Dispersive_Tree_Agreements_for_Multi-Robot_Coordination|Dispersive Tree Agreements for Multi-Robot Coordination]]

![[assets/2610.31540_figure.png|800]]

- **PDF**: https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.31540

#### 研究背景与动机

《Dispersive Tree Agreements for Multi-Robot Coordination》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Robotics 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：现有方法仍面临挑战；现有方法存在能力或适用范围限制；系统成本或推理开销是关键约束。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；提出新的模型、框架或算法；给出系统化方法或工具。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性；关注鲁棒性或泛化表现。 可见文本未明确列出完整数据集名称。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Multi-robot coordination of nonlinear systems is naturally formulated as joint-space optimal control subject to constraints over groups of robots. Existing methods that approach joint optimality remain largely centralized, offline, and confined to collision-free motion planning, while decentralized, real-time counterparts target safe navigation without pursuing the joint optimum. This paper develops a decentralized, real-time planner that approaches the joint optimum as an anytime process. We extend a recent single-robot framework that searches dispersive trees of forward-propagated commands for differentially flat systems to coordinate a multi-robot team near-optimally. We first show that the inter-robot coupling can be effectively replaced by some local constraints, under which locally grown dispersive trees contain a near-optimal agreement. It is this structure that the robots pursue by decentralized column generation, negotiating rents on the shared space that guide the search of each robot's tree. The negotiation settles on an agreement whose near-optimality is certified at quiescence. Our protocol, WarpWeaver, implements this negotiation as an asynchronous real-time loop over a receding horizon, and demonstrates effective closure to quiesced agreements even when real-time deadlines cut the negotiation short. In simulation, WarpWeaver reduces total travel time by up to 22% against centralized offline planners on the Dynobench suite and by up to 37% against a decentralized real-time baseline, and nearly doubles the success rate of a task-specific planner on graph connectivity. Hardware experiments on six robots planning onboard demonstrate real-world implementability.

</details>

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；提出新的模型、框架或算法；设计端到端框架。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Task and Motion Planning (TAMP) stands as a fundamental challenge in robotics, essential for enabling autonomous agents to execute complex, long-horizon tasks in diverse environments. While Large Language Models (LLMs) introduce a new paradigm for TAMP by leveraging extensive common sense, their inherent reasoning limitations hinder direct application, and fine-tuning strategies to enhance these capabilities are currently bottlenecked by the scarcity of high-quality datasets. Existing automated generation methods often suffer from homogeneity, limiting model generalization, and unsolvability, where hallucinated scene graphs create physical mismatches with task goals. To address these issues, we propose Scene-graph Construction Optimized by Plan Error-correction (SCOPE), an automated framework designed to generate diverse and executable TAMP datasets. SCOPE incorporates multi-level diversity metrics to maximize the information entropy of scenarios. Crucially, it introduces a Plan Feedback Error-correction pipeline that utilizes planner execution failures as feedback signals to retroactively rectify logical inconsistencies in scene graphs, ensuring physical feasibility. Experimental results demonstrate that SCOPE exhibits greater robustness to erroneous scene graphs and generates higher-quality data. When utilized for LLM fine-tuning, SCOPE-generated data yield a downstream TAMP model that achieves an absolute 12.33% gain in success rate over its counterpart fine-tuned with data generated by DELTA, a state-of-the-art PDDL-based planner.

</details>

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：实验或分析展示了方法有效性；结果验证了方案可行性；通过评测分析了方法表现。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：LARYBench, Olaf-World。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Robot world models simulate how environments evolve in response to robot actions. Latent action models enable learning dynamics from videos by encoding visual transitions as proxy actions. However, reconstruction-driven latent actions mix robot motion with object responses and other scene changes, complicating their correspondence with robot controls. We propose SkeLAM, a skeleton-guided latent action model for controllable robot world modeling. SkeLAM decomposes latent actions into a kinematic component grounded by robot skeleton supervision and a residual component retaining complementary visual dynamics. Cross-modal swap reconstruction encourages RGB-derived and skeleton-derived kinematic representations to be interchangeable during video reconstruction. The world model incorporates both components through separate conditioning pathways, while branch dropout reduces its dependence on future-derived residual information. A lightweight adapter maps robot actions to the kinematic space, and subsequent world-model fine-tuning adapts generation to these predicted conditions. At inference, the model generates future observations from the current observation and robot actions. On DROID, SkeLAM reduces end-effector ADE by 19.5% and LPIPS by 11.1% compared with DreamDojo. Motion probing supports kinematic specialization, while qualitative results demonstrate zero-shot human-to-robot motion transfer in generated rollouts. Policy evaluation shows agreement between predicted success rates and those measured in real robot executions.

</details>

方法上，论文主要涉及：构建或使用基准评测体系；设计端到端框架；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能、大模型 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析

实验结果方面，可见文本显示：在目标指标上带来改进；达到作者报告的目标性能；实验或分析展示了方法有效性。 可见文本中出现的评测对象/数据集包括：MARL。 如果需要进一步判断价值，应继续核对原文中的主实验表、消融实验和失败案例。

<details>
<summary>完整摘要</summary>

Robotic swarms must coordinate across diverse missions, yet existing controllers often require redesign, retuning, or retraining when objectives change. Here we show that probabilistic energy modeling provides a unifying representation for versatile swarm control, in which diverse missions emerge from compositions of shared interaction principles rather than task-specific control formulations. By exploiting the correspondence between Gibbs random field energy minimization and structured reward maximization, we encode fundamental swarm behaviors as reusable energy terms within a unified probabilistic objective. Decentralized policies trained within this representation are composed into a single controller through lightweight policy gating, without retraining. An interpretable action-attention mechanism captures non-uniform local interactions, while dimensionless normalization supports transfer across swarm scales and platforms. Demonstrated on aerial swarms, the unified policy generalizes zero-shot across six mission classes, 10-100 agents, two hardware platforms, and indoor cluttered and GPS-denied woodland environments. Large-scale simulations achieve 0.96-1.00 task success rates with 1-10 ms per-agent inference latency, while physical deployments confirm zero-shot transfer to real platforms and unstructured environments. These results establish probabilistic energy modeling as a scalable and transferable framework for decentralized swarm control.

</details>

《Learning Robot Behavior Priors from Non-Robotic Synthetic Data》归入 机器人、具身智能 方向。该论文围绕 Machine Learning 中的具体任务展开，重点关注方法在真实场景、复杂环境或高可靠性要求下的表现。从摘要和可见正文判断，研究动机主要来自：相关基准、数据或方法仍不充分。当前为无 LLM 兜底摘要，未直接粘贴英文证据句。

#### 方法概述和架构

方法上，论文主要涉及：引入数据集或数据收集流程；使用 Transformer/基础模型结构；引入智能体式建模或搜索。分析时应重点检查方法名称、输入输出、核心模块、训练或推理流程，以及这些模块如何服务于 机器人、具身智能 场景。由于当前未启用 LLM 深读，这里只给出结构化中文兜底，不保留英文原句。

#### 实验结果分析
